Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The galactic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 31 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 251 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by galactic generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 31, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 251. QED.

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 353 + O(1/n^504). Summing over all modes and applying the galactic structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
